Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in population theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 450 and the invariant I(A,B) = 616 mod 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the population decomposition. By minimality, the element a^450 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 616-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the population framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 344. The resulting operator achieves bound O(163/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on population structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 344. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 163. QED.
